I_{2}=cLM^{2}\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}\frac{\eta_{k}}{|\mathcal{U}_{k}|^{2}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}\max_{\ell=1,2,\ldots,d}\left({\rm Corr}^{(\ell)}_{i,i^{\prime},k}\right)\leq cLM^{2}\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}{\eta_{k}}\bar{\rho}_{k}

(62)

where
\bar{\rho}_{k}=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{U}_{k}|^{2}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}|\max_{\ell=1,2,\ldots,d}\left({\rm Corr}^{(\ell)}_{i,i^{\prime},k}\right)|
.

For term
I_{3}
: Notice the bias term
\|{\rm Bias}_{k}\|
is upper bounded by a convergent sequence based on Theorem
3
and Eq. (
25
). For
R\in\{LR,CLR\}

\displaystyle\|{\rm Bias}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Z_{1}^{R}(t+1)}{|\mathcal{U}_{k}|}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}+2M\varphi(nt)+\frac{Z_{2}^{R}}{|\mathcal{U}_{k}|}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\in\mathcal{U}_{k}}\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}

\displaystyle\leq Z_{1}^{R}\eta_{1}\frac{(t+1)t}{(k-B_{k}-t)^{r}}+Z_{2}^{R}\eta_{1}\frac{B_{k}+t}{(k-B_{k}-t)^{r}}+2M\varphi(nt)\xrightarrow{k\rightarrow\infty}0

(63)

where the step (
63
) holds because
\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\leq\eta_{1}t(i-t)^{-r}\leq\eta_{1}t(k-B_{k}-t)^{-r}
and
\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\leq\eta_{1}(k-i+t)(i-t)^{-r}\leq\eta_{1}(B_{k}+t)(k-B_{k}-t)^{-r}
. The convergence of a sequence implies that it is bounded. Thus, we can bound the norm of bias as
\|{\rm Bias}_{k}\|\leq\sup_{k\geq 1}\|{\rm Bias}_{k}\|
.
By applying
\sum^{K}_{k=B_{K}+t}\eta_{k}=\sum^{K}_{k=B_{K}+t}\frac{\eta_{1}}{k^{r}}\leq\int^{K-B_{K}-t}_{0}\frac{\eta_{1}}{k^{r}}\mathrm{d}k\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{1-r}(K-B_{K}-t)^{1-r}
and the bias bound, we can show

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq{C_{3}}(K-B_{K}-t)^{1-r}

where
C_{3}=\sup_{k\geq 1}\|{\rm Bias}_{k}\|
.
Then putting it all together, dividing both sides by
\frac{1}{2}(K-B_{K}-t+1)
gives the result

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]}{K-B_{K}-t+1}\leq\frac{4\eta_{1}^{-1}U_{J}K^{r}}{K-B_{K}-t+1}+\frac{2cLM^{2}}{K-B_{k}-t+1}\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}{\eta_{k}}\bar{\rho}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2C_{3}\eta_{1}}{1-r}(K-B_{K}-t)^{-r}+{4M^{2}}\varphi(nt)+\frac{2C_{2}\eta_{1}(t+1)t}{1-r}(K-B_{K}-t)^{-r}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2C_{1}\eta_{1}}{1-r}(B_{K}+t)(K-B_{K}-t)^{-r}.

(64)

For large enough
K
such that
K\geq 2t+2B_{K}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{4U_{J}K^{r}}{K-B_{K}-t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4U_{J}K^{r}}{K-B_{K}-t}\leq\frac{8U_{J}}{K^{1-r}}

\displaystyle(K-B_{K}-t)^{-r}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{r}}{K^{r}}.

Therefore, Eq. (
64
) becomes

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]}{K-B_{K}-t+1}\leq\frac{8U_{J}/\eta_{1}}{K^{1-r}}+\frac{2cLM^{2}}{K-B_{k}-t+1}\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}{\eta_{k}}\bar{\rho}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2^{r+1}C_{3}\eta_{1}}{1-r}K^{-r}+{4M^{2}}\varphi(nt)+\frac{2^{r+1}C_{2}\eta_{1}(t+1)t}{1-r}K^{-r}+\frac{2^{r+1}C_{1}\eta_{1}}{1-r}(B_{K}+t)K^{-r}.

Notice that the learning rate is positive
\eta_{k}>0
. Since
\bar{\rho}_{k}>0
, when
K\geq 2t+2B_{K}
, we have

\frac{2cLM^{2}}{K-B_{k}-t+1}\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}{\eta_{k}}\bar{\rho}_{k}\leq\frac{4cLM^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\eta_{k}}\bar{\rho}_{k}.

By applying Lemma
22
with
f(K)=B_{K}+t
and
U_{a}=M^{2}
, we have the first conclusion

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{k=B_{K}+t}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]}{K-B_{K}-t+1}+M^{2}\frac{B_{K}+t}{K}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8U_{J}/\eta_{1}}{K^{1-r}}+\frac{4cLM^{2}}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\eta_{k}}\bar{\rho}_{k}+{4M^{2}}\varphi(nt)+\frac{2^{r+1}C_{3}\eta_{1}}{(1-r)K^{r}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2^{r+1}C_{2}\eta_{1}(t+1)t}{(1-r)K^{r}}+\frac{2^{r+1}C_{1}\eta_{1}}{1-r}\frac{B_{K}+t}{K^{r}}+M^{2}\frac{B_{K}+t}{K}.

(65)

Then writing Eq. (
65
) using
\mathcal{O}(\cdot)
notation gives the second conclusion

\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{K^{1-r}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sum^{K}_{k=1}\eta_{k}\bar{\rho}_{k}}{K}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\varphi(nt)\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{t^{2}}{K^{r}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{B_{K}+t}{K^{r}}\right)\\

which completes the proof.
∎

D.5
Proof of Corollary
3

Corollary
3

Suppose Assumptions
A.1
and
A.2
hold. Under the same configurations as Theorem
5
, by setting
t=\log_{\kappa}K^{-r/n}
, we have the rate of convergence

\frac{1}{K}\sum^{K}_{k=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{K^{1-r}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{K^{r}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{t^{2}}{K^{r}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{B_{K}+t}{K^{r}}\right)

where the notation

\mathcal{O}(\cdot)
hides constants
c
,
L
,
\eta_{1}
,
M
,
U_{J}
,
n
,
U_{f}
,
U_{\pi}
,
U_{L}
,
\kappa_{0}
,
\kappa
and
L_{g}
.

Proof.

For the dynamic buffer with size
B_{K}>0
, it holds that
K-i\leq B_{K}
for any
\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}\in\mathcal{U}_{K}
. Here the buffer size is an increasing function of
K
. By setting
t=\log_{\kappa}K^{-r/n}
, it holds that

\varphi(nt)=\kappa_{0}\kappa^{n\log_{\kappa}\left(\frac{1}{K^{r}}\right)^{1/n}}=\kappa_{0}\kappa^{\log_{\kappa}\left(\frac{1}{K^{r}}\right)}=\kappa_{0}K^{-r}.

The conclusion is obtained by applying Theorem
5
.
∎

Appendix E
Justification of MBB Estimator of Gradient Variance

According to
Lahiri 2003
, there are two conditions required for the consistency of the MBB variance estimators (
30
) and (
31
): there exists a
\delta>0
such that (1)
{\mathbb{E}}[\|g_{i,j}\|^{2+\delta}]<\infty
and that (2)
\sum^{\infty}_{j=1}\alpha(j)^{\delta/2+\delta}<\infty
where
\alpha(j)
represents the strong mixing coefficient; see the definition in
Lahiri 2003
. Without loss of generality, we show both conditions hold when
\delta=2/3
.

Condition (1)
holds due to

{\mathbb{E}}[\|g_{i,j}\|^{8/3}]\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\right|^{8/3}\|g\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)\|^{8/3}\right]\leq U_{L}^{8/3}M^{8/3}<\infty

by applying Assumption
A.1
and Lemma
2
.

Condition (2)
: For a
fixed
behavior policy
\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}
and target policy
\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}
, the time-invariant state transition probability defines a stationary Markov chain
\{(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)})\}_{j=1}^{n}
, with transition probability
p(\boldsymbol{s}^{\prime},\boldsymbol{a}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\vcentcolon=p(\boldsymbol{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{s}^{\prime})
. For such a MC, Assumption
A.2
implies the uniform ergodicity, i.e., for
\forall j\geq 1,\forall\boldsymbol{s}\in\mathcal{S}
it holds

\left\|\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,1)}=\boldsymbol{s})\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)})-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{TV}=\left\|\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,1)}=\boldsymbol{s})-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}}(\cdot)\right\|_{TV}\leq\kappa_{0}\kappa^{j}

where
d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})=d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}}(\boldsymbol{s})\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})
. It is known that for stationary Markov chains,
the geometric ergodicity implies the
\beta
-mixing
(
Bradley 2007
, Thm 21.19)
and the
\beta
-mixing implies strong-mixing
(
Bradley 2005
)
.

Since the gradient estimates
g_{i,j}
are measurable functions of state-action pair
(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)})
, for the LR gradient estimate based on samples from
i
-th iteration, the sigma-algebra, denoted by
\sigma(\cdot)
, generated by the gradient estimates is a subset of that generated by the state-action samples, i.e.,
\sigma(\{g_{i,j}\}_{j=1}^{n})\subset\sigma(\{(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)})\}_{j=1}^{n})
. Then, the sequence
\{g_{i,j}\}_{j=1}^{n}
is strong-mixing due to the fact as
n\rightarrow\infty

\displaystyle\alpha(n)

\displaystyle=\sup_{j}\alpha\left(\sigma\left(\{g_{i,j^{\prime}}\}_{j^{\prime}=1}^{j}\right),\sigma\left(\{g_{i,j^{\prime}}\}_{j^{\prime}=j+n}^{\infty}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{j}\alpha\left(\sigma\left(\{(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j^{\prime})})\}_{j^{\prime}=1}^{j}\right),\sigma\left(\{(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j^{\prime})},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j^{\prime})})\}_{j^{\prime}=j+n}^{\infty}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{j}\beta\left(\sigma\left(\{(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j^{\prime})})\}_{j^{\prime}=1}^{j}\right),\sigma\left(\{(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j^{\prime})},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j^{\prime})})\}_{j^{\prime}=j+n}^{\infty}\right)\right)

(66)

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{0}\kappa^{n}

(67)

where step (
66
) holds due to Eq. (1.11) in
Bradley 2005
and step (
67
) holds due to
Bradley 2007
. Therefore,
Condition (2)
follows that

\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\alpha(j)=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\kappa_{0}\frac{\kappa-\kappa^{n+1}}{1-\kappa}=\frac{\kappa_{0}\kappa}{1-\kappa}<\infty.

Appendix F
Justification for the Variance Ratio Approximation

Proposition
2
.

Under Assumptions
A.1
and
A.2
, for any
t
such that
t<i\leq k
, if
t=o(k^{r/2})
and
B_{k}=o(k^{r/2})
, the total variance ratio of the individual LR policy gradient estimator and PG estimator has the approximation

\frac{{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}_{i,k}\right]\right)}{{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right)}\approx e^{{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\rm{KL}}\left(\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})\|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})\right)\right]}\left(1+\zeta_{k}^{-1}\right)-\zeta^{-1}_{k}

where
\zeta_{k}={{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right)}/{\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right\|^{2}}
is the relative variance.

Proof.

Consider the total variance of LR policy gradient estimator for a state-action pair
(\boldsymbol{s}^{i,j},\boldsymbol{a}^{i,j})
. Let
{\mathbb{E}}_{i}[\cdot]={\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[\cdot]
. We have

\displaystyle{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}_{i,k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{i}\left[\left\|\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]-\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{i}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad-\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\exp\left\{\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\exp\left\{\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|^{2}

(68)

where the approximation (
68
) becomes valid when iteration
i
is large enough such that the total variation distance between the
ni
-step and
nk
-step transition distributions is approaching to zero; see Lemma
18
for detailed justification.

By a second-order Taylor expansion of
{\mathbb{E}}[\log(X)]
around
\mu={\mathbb{E}}(X)
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}[\log(X)]\approx{\mathbb{E}}\left[\log{\mathbb{E}}[X]+\frac{X-{\mathbb{E}}[X]}{{\mathbb{E}}[X]}-\frac{(X-{\mathbb{E}}[X])^{2}}{2{\mathbb{E}}[X]^{2}}\right]=\log{\mathbb{E}}[X]-\frac{{\rm Var}[X]}{2{\mathbb{E}}[X]^{2}}.

(69)

Let
{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[\cdot]={\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[\cdot]
. Let
q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})=\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}
. By applying Taylor approximation (
69
) to the first term of (
68
),
we get

\displaystyle{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}_{i,k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\approx\exp\left\{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\log\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\right)\right]+{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\log\left(\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right)\right]+\frac{{\rm Var}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]}{2{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|^{2}.

Applying the Taylor approximation (
69
) to
{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\log\left(\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right)\right]
gives

\displaystyle{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}_{i,k}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\approx\exp\left\{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\log\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\right)\right]+\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]\right)\right\}\mathcal{G}-\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\exp\left\{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\log\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\right)\right]\right\}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]\mathcal{G}-\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|^{2}

where
\log\mathcal{G}=\frac{{\rm Var}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]}{2{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}-\frac{{\rm Var}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]}{2{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}\right)
is the second-order approximation error induced by Taylor expansion. From Lemma
19
, we know that
\mathcal{G}\rightarrow 1
as
k\rightarrow\infty
. Thus we can approximate the total variance ratio by

\displaystyle\frac{{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}_{i,k}\right]\right)}{{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right)}

\displaystyle\approx e^{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mbox{KL}\left(\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})\|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})\right)\right]}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[\left\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|^{2}]}{{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right)}-\frac{\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|^{2}}{{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right)}

\displaystyle\approx e^{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\mbox{KL}\left(\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})\|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})\right)\right]}\left(1+\zeta_{k}^{-1}\right)-\zeta^{-1}_{k}

where
\zeta_{k}={{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right)}/{\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right\|^{2}}
is the relative variance.
∎

Appendix G
Proof of Technical Lemmas

Lemma 9
.

Under Assumption
A.1
-
A.2
, for any
t
such that
t<i\leq k
we have

(i)

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right\|\leq M(U_{L}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
;

(ii)

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]\leq M(U_{L}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
;

(iii)

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{CLR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J^{CLR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]\leq M(U_{f}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
.

Proof.

(i)
and
(ii)
: Let

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(i)}}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(ii)}}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right].

It follows that

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(ii)}}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left.\left.\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\quad-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\left|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\right|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right].

(70)

By Assumption
A.1
, we have
\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)/\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\leq U_{L}
. By applying Lemma
7
, we have
\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\|\leq L_{g}\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|
. By Lemma
2
, we have
\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\|\leq M
. Then the conclusion follows that

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(i)}}\leq\Delta_{\textbf{(ii)}}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

(71)

\displaystyle\leq U_{L}L_{g}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|\right]+MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|]

(72)

\displaystyle\leq(U_{L}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})M\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}

where Step (
71
) follows (
70
), the last step holds by Lemma
20
. The first term of Step (
72
) holds due to Assumption
A.1
and Lemma
7
. The second term of Step (
72
) holds because

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle=M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\right|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|\right].

The last step holds by applying (
8
) in Assumption
A.1
.

(iii)
The proof of
(iii)
is similar to that of
(i)
and
(ii)
.
Bound the likelihood ratio by
\min\left\{\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)},U_{f}\right\}\leq U_{f}
, and notice that for any
(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
, it holds that

\min\left\{\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right\}-\min\left\{\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right\}\leq\min\left\{\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right\}.

Then by applying Lemma
7
and Assumption
A.1
, the conclusion follows that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{CLR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J^{CLR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)},U_{f}\right\}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)},U_{f}\right\}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq U_{f}L_{g}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|\right]+MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|]

\displaystyle\leq(U_{f}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})M\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

∎

Lemma 10
.

Under Assumption
A.1
-
A.2
, for any
t
such that
t<i\leq k
, we have

(i)

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right\|\leq 2nM^{2}U_{\pi}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
;

(ii)

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]\leq 2M+2M^{2}U_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
;

(iii)

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{CLR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right\|\leq 4nM^{2}U_{\pi}U_{L}U_{f}(t+1)\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
;

(iv)

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{CLR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]\leq(3U_{f}+1)M
.

Proof.

(i)
: Let
\Delta_{\textbf{(i)}}=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|
.

Conditioning on
\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}
and
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(i)}}

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\int\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.-\int\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\int\left(\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\mathbb{P}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\int\left|\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\mathbb{P}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\leq M\int\left|\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\mathbb{P}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right|\int\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

(73)

\displaystyle\leq 2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

(74)

where Step (
73
) holds due to Lemma
2
and the last inequality is by the definition of total variation. Let

\Delta^{(i,j)}=2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}.

By Lemma
3
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{(i,j)}

\displaystyle\leq 2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j-1)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j-1)}\right)\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j-1)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j-1)}\right)\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\leq 2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j-1)}\in\cdot\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j-1)}\in\cdot\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\quad+2MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

\displaystyle={\Delta^{(i,j-1)}}+2MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

(75)

\displaystyle\cdots

\displaystyle={\Delta^{(i-1,n)}}+2MU_{\pi}\sum^{j}_{j^{\prime}=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right].

In sum, repeating applying the inequality (
75
) from
(i,j)
to
(i-t,1)
gives

\displaystyle\Delta^{(i,j)}\leq\ldots

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{(i-t,1)}+2MU_{\pi}\sum^{j}_{j^{\prime}=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]+2MU_{\pi}\sum^{i-1}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}]

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{(i-t,1)}+2nMU_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

where the last step holds as
\sum^{j}_{j^{\prime}=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\leq n{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]
. By noting

\Delta^{(i-t,j)}=2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i-t,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}\right)\right\|_{TV}=0\text{ for any $j\in[n]$}.

Thus we have
\Delta^{(i,j)}\leq 2nMU_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}]
.

By Lemma
20
, we have
{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}]\leq M\sum^{i^{\prime}}_{\ell=i-t}{\eta_{\ell}}
and thus it holds that

\Delta_{\textbf{(i)}}\leq\Delta^{(i,j)}\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}\sum^{i}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}\sum^{i^{\prime}}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}

(76)

Then the conclusion follows from the fact that
{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{\textbf{(i)}}]\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
.

(ii)
: Different from
(i)
, we define the conditional expectation

\Delta_{\textbf{(ii)}}={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right].

It follows that

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(ii)}}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}g\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}g\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left({\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|g\left({\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right].

Let
p\left(\boldsymbol{\tau}\right)=p\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j+1)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)
denote the probability density of sample path from
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}
to
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}
. Conditioning on
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}
and
\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}
, then we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(ii)}}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]+2M

\displaystyle\leq M\int p\left(\boldsymbol{\tau}\right)\left(\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{\tau}+2M

\displaystyle\leq M\int p\left(\boldsymbol{\tau}\right)\left(\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\right|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{\tau}+2M

\displaystyle\leq 2M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\right\|_{TV}\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]+2M

(77)

\displaystyle\leq 2MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]+2M

(78)

\displaystyle\leq 2M^{2}U_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}+2M

(79)

where the expectation in Step (
77
) is taken over the sample path from
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}
to
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}
. Step (
78
) holds by Assumption
A.1
and Step (
79
) follows Lemma
20
. The conclusion follows by the fact that

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{LR}\left({\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]\leq 2M^{2}U_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}+2M.

(iii)
: We define conditional expectation

\Delta_{\textbf{(iii)}}=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)},U_{f}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|.

Let
\mathcal{E}=\left\{\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\leq U_{f}\right\}
denote the event that the likelihood ratio is less than the maximum value
U_{f}
. Conditioning on
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}
and
\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}
, it holds

\displaystyle\Delta_{\textbf{(iii)}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)},U_{f}\right)\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}-1,U_{f}-1\right)g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.U_{f}\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})\right\|

(80)

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\right\|

(81)

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{\left|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\right|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

(82)

where the term (
82
) holds since
\min(a,b)\leq a
for any
a
and
b
.
For term (
80
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.U_{f}\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\leq U_{f}\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad-\left.\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right\|\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})

\displaystyle\quad\leq U_{f}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\|g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2MU_{f}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})\left\|\mathbb{P}\left((\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left((\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

(83)

where Step (
83
) holds due to Lemma
2
. Let

\Delta^{(i,j)}=2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left((\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left((\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}.

By Lemma
3
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{(i,j)}

\displaystyle\leq 2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\quad+2MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{(i,j-1)}+2MU_{\pi}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right].

(84)

Repeat the inequality (
84
) from
(i,j)
to
(i-t,j)
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{(i,j)}\leq\ldots

\displaystyle\leq\Delta^{(i-t,j)}+2MU_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}]

\displaystyle=\Delta^{(i-t,j)}+2nMU_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right].

Notice that

\Delta^{(i-t,j)}=2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i-t,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})\right\|_{TV}=0.

Thus we have
\Delta^{(i,j)}\leq 2nMU_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}]
.
By Lemma
20
, we have
{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i^{\prime}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\||\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}]\leq M\sum^{i^{\prime}}_{\ell=i-t}{\eta_{\ell}}
and thus it holds that

\Delta^{(i,j)}\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}\sum^{i}_{i^{\prime}=i-t}\sum^{i^{\prime}}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

Then the term (
80
) is bounded by

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.U_{f}\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})\right\|\leq 2U_{f}nU_{\pi}M^{2}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

(85)

For term (
81
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\right\|

\displaystyle\ \leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})\right.

\displaystyle\quad-\left.\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\left(\int_{\Theta}\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{\theta}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)^{2}}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}\right)\right|\|g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\ \leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})\right.

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})+\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})

\displaystyle\quad-\left.\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)^{2}}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}\right|\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right|\|g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\ \leq M\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\quad+M\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\int_{\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)\right|\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right]

\displaystyle\ \leq 2M\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\left\|\mathbb{P}\left((\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left((\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\quad+MU_{L}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\int_{\mathcal{S}}\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)\right|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right]

(86)

\displaystyle\ \leq 2M\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\left\|\mathbb{P}\left((\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left((\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)})\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\qquad+2MU_{L}U_{\pi}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|\right]

(87)

where the step (
86
) follows Lemma
3
and the last step follows
A.1
. By Eq. (
76
), the first term of (
87
) is bounded by
2nU_{\pi}M^{2}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
. By Lemma
20
, we have
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|\right]\leq M\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
. Putting them together gives

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left[g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\leq 2M^{2}U_{\pi}nU_{f}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}+2M^{2}U_{\pi}U_{L}\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

(88)

For term (
82
), based on the similar steps as Eq. (
79
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\frac{\left|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)\right|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2M^{2}U_{\pi}\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

(89)

By putting (
85
), (
88
) and (
89
) all together, we have

\Delta_{\textbf{(iii)}}\leq 4nM^{2}U_{\pi}U_{L}U_{f}(t+1)\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

(90)

The conclusion follows the fact that
{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta_{\textbf{(iii)}}]\leq 4nM^{2}U_{\pi}U_{L}U_{f}(t+1)\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

(iv):
Notice that for any
\boldsymbol{s},\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}\in\mathcal{S}
and
\boldsymbol{a},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}\in\mathcal{A}
, it holds

\displaystyle\left\|\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{|}\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{x}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{x}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right)g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right)g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right)\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|+\left|\min\left(\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)},U_{f}\right)-1\right|\|g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\|

\displaystyle\leq 2U_{f}M+(U_{f}+1)M

from which the conclusion immediately follows.
∎

Lemma 11
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, for any
t
such that
t<i\leq k
, we have

(i)

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right\|\leq 2M\varphi(nt)
;

(ii)

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]\leq 2M.

Proof.

(i)
Let
\Delta=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|
. It holds

\displaystyle\Delta

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\check{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\check{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\int\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\int d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s})\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\int\left(p\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s})\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\int\left|p\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s})\right|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\leq M\int\left|p\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s})\right|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

(91)

\displaystyle=M\int\left|p\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}=\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s})\right|\left(\int\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right)\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}

\displaystyle=2M\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\cdot)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\leq 2M\varphi(nt).

(92)

Then the conclusion follows from the fact that

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right\|\leq 2M\varphi(nt).

(ii)
The conclusion can be obtained by observing that

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|g\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\tilde{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-g\left(\check{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)},\check{\boldsymbol{a}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|\right]\leq{\mathbb{E}}[2M]=2M,

which concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 12
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, for any
t
such that
t<i\leq k
, we have

\|{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]\|\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\Delta\|\right]\leq L_{g}M\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}

where
\Delta=\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)
.

Proof.

By Lemma
7
,

{\mathbb{E}}[\|\Delta\|]={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|g(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-g(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|\right]\leq L_{g}{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|].

The conclusion follows by applying Lemma
20
.
∎

Lemma 13
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, for any
t
such that
t<i\leq k
, we have

(i)

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\nabla J\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)\right]\right\|\leq 2M^{2}C_{d}\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}
;

(ii)

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\nabla J\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)\right\|\right]\leq 2M.

Proof.

(i)
:
Let
\Delta={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\nabla J\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{i-t,j},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]
. For notational simplicity, we omit the conditions on
(\boldsymbol{s}^{i-t,j},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})
below. Then we have

\displaystyle\|\Delta\|

\displaystyle=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\sim d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\cdot,\cdot)}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\sim d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}}(\cdot,\cdot)}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\int d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}-\int d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\int|d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})|\left\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\right\|_{TV}\right]

(93)

\displaystyle\leq 2MC_{d}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right\|\right]

(94)

where
C_{d}=U_{\pi}(1+\lceil\log_{\kappa}\kappa_{0}^{-1}\rceil+\frac{1}{1-\kappa})
and
\kappa\geq 1
. Step (
93
) holds due to Lemma
2
and Step (
94
) is by Lemma
4
. The conclusion is obtained by applying Lemma
20
, i.e.

\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\nabla J\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)\right]\right\|=\|{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]\|\leq{\mathbb{E}}[\|\Delta\|]\leq 2M^{2}C_{d}\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

(ii)
: The conclusion is obtained by Minkowski’s inequality and Lemma
2
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\nabla J\left(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|g(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-g(\boldsymbol{x}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|\right]\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|g(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|+\left\|g(\boldsymbol{x}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|\right]\leq 2M

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 14
.

For a learning rate
\eta_{k}=\eta_{1}k^{-r}
with
\eta_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4L}
and
r\in(0,1)
, under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, by following the selection rule defined in Theorem
4
, we have

\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Gamma\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\Gamma\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\leq C^{\Gamma}_{1}(k-i+t)^{1/2}\eta_{i-t}+C_{2}^{\Gamma}(k-i+t)\eta_{i-t}

where
C_{1}^{\Gamma}={LM^{2}\sqrt{2c+1}(\sqrt{2c+1}+2)}
and
C_{2}^{\Gamma}=M^{2}(\max\{U_{L},U_{f}\}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})
.

Proof.

Let
\Delta=\frac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\left(\Gamma\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)-\Gamma\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right)
.
Recall that

\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}=\frac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\text{ and }\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,i-t}=\frac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}).

where
R\in\{LR,CLR\}
. Then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}),\frac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\frac{1}{n}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2}+\Delta_{3}

(95)

where

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\Delta_{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\Delta_{3}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,i-t}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right].

By Minkowski’s inequality, the norm
|{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]|\leq|\Delta_{1}|+|\Delta_{2}|+|\Delta_{3}|
.

(i)
For the term
|\Delta_{1}|
:

\displaystyle|\Delta_{1}|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}

(96)

\displaystyle\leq L{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|^{2}]^{1/2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}

(97)

where Step (
96
) follows by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and Step (
97
) holds due to Lemma
1
. For the first term of (
97
), applying Minkowski’s inequality and Lemma
2
gives

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{\ell=i-t+1}^{k}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell-1}\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{\ell=i-t+1}^{k}\eta_{\ell}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{\ell}\right\|^{2}\right].

(98)

Theorem
4
implies that

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{\ell}\right\|^{2}\right]={\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{\ell}\right]\right)+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{\ell}\right]\right\|^{2}\leq c{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{\ell}\right]\right)+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{\ell}\right]\right\|^{2}.

(99)

Also by Minkowski’s inequality, it holds for any
\ell
that

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{\ell}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{|\mathcal{U}_{\ell}|n}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\in\mathcal{U}_{\ell}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{M}{|\mathcal{U}_{\ell}|n}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\in\mathcal{U}_{\ell}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq M.

(100)

By Lemma
2
, it follows that

{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{\ell}\right]\right)={\mathbb{E}}[\|g(\boldsymbol{x};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell})\|^{2}]-\|{\mathbb{E}}[g(\boldsymbol{x};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell})]\|^{2}\leq 2M^{2}.

(101)

By collecting (
99
), (
100
) and (
101
), Eq. (
98
) becomes

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|^{2}\right]\leq(2c+1)M^{2}\sum_{\ell=i-t}^{k}\eta_{\ell}^{2}\leq(2c+1)M^{2}(k-i+t)\eta_{i-t}^{2}

where the last inequality holds because
\{\eta_{k}\}
is a non-increasing sequence.
Then the first term of Step (
97
) becomes

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}\leq\sqrt{2c+1}M(k-i+t)^{1/2}\eta_{i-t}.

(102)

For the second term of Step (
97
), applying Minkowski’s inequality and Lemma
2
gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}+M.

By applying
Selection Rule 1
defined in Theorem
4
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}\right\|^{2}\right]{=}{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}\right]\right)+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}\right]\right\|^{2}\leq c{\rm Tr}\left({\rm Var}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{PG}_{k}\right]\right)+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}\right]\right\|^{2}.\\

(103)

By substituting (
100
) and (
101
) into (
103
), we have
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq(2c+1)M^{2}
and consequently, the second term of Step (
97
) becomes

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}_{i,k}-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}\leq M(\sqrt{2c+1}+1).

Then we have

|\Delta_{1}|\leq L(2c+1+\sqrt{2c+1})M^{2}(k-i+t)^{1/2}\eta_{i-t}.

(ii)
For the term
|\Delta_{2}|
:

\displaystyle|\Delta_{2}|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\right\rangle\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq LM{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|^{2}\right]^{1/2}

(104)

\displaystyle\leq L\sqrt{2c+1}M^{2}(k-i+t)^{1/2}\eta_{i-t}

where the first inequality follows by applying Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and Lemma
1
and Step (
104
) is due to Lemma
2
. The last step is held by using the bound (
102
).

(iii)
For the term
|\Delta_{3}|
:

\displaystyle|\Delta_{3}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left.\left.\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left.{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\rangle\right|\right]

(105)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\|\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|\right]

(106)

\displaystyle\leq M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|\right]

(107)

\displaystyle\leq M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})-\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\|\right]

(108)

where Step (
105
) and (
108
) both follows Jensen’s inequality, Step (
106
) follows Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Step (
107
) holds by applying Lemma
2
.

By applying Lemma
9
, we have

|\Delta_{3}|=\begin{cases}M^{2}(U_{L}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}&\text{R $=$ LR};\\
M^{2}(U_{f}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}&\text{R $=$ CLR}.\end{cases}

(109)

Because
\{\eta_{k}\}
is a non-increasing sequence, it holds that
\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\leq(k-i+t)\eta_{i-t}
and thus we have

|\Delta_{3}|\leq M^{2}(\max\{U_{L},U_{f}\}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\leq M^{2}(\max\{U_{L},U_{f}\}L_{g}+MU_{\pi})(k-i+t)\eta_{i-t}.

The conclusion is obtained by summing
|\Delta_{1}|
,
|\Delta_{2}|
and
|\Delta_{3}|
.
∎

Lemma 15
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, we have

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Gamma\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\Gamma\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}2nM^{3}U_{\pi}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}&\text{LR estimator};\\
4nM^{3}U_{\pi}U_{L}U_{f}(t+1)\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}&\text{CLR estimator}.\end{cases}

Proof.

Let
\Delta=\Gamma\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\Gamma\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)
. For both LR and CLR policy gradient estimators, i.e.
R\in\{LR,CLR\}
, we have

\displaystyle|{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left.\left.\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\rangle\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J^{R}\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|\right]

(110)

where the first inequality holds by applying Jensen’s inequality and Step (
110
) is by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and Lemma
2
.
Then the conclusion follows by using Eq. (
76
) and Eq. (
90
).
∎

Lemma 16
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, we have

\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Gamma\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\Gamma\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right]\right|\leq 2M^{2}\varphi(nt).

Proof.

Let
\Delta=\Gamma\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\Gamma\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)
. We have

\displaystyle|{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]|

\displaystyle=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right.\right.\right.
