\displaystyle\qquad-\left.\left.\left.\left.\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),\widehat{\nabla}J(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\rangle\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\rangle\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left.\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\|\right]

(111)

where Step (
111
) holds by applying Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and Lemma
2
.
Then the conclusion immediately follows by applying Eq. (
92
).

∎

Lemma 17
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Gamma\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)\right]=0.

Proof.

Since the sample
\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)}
is drawn from the stationary distribution
d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\boldsymbol{x})
, the gradient estimate
\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)
is unbiased, that is,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Gamma\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}\right)|\boldsymbol{s}^{i-t,j},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}),{\mathbb{E}}\left[\widehat{\nabla}J\left(\check{\boldsymbol{x}}^{(i,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\nabla J(\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})|\boldsymbol{s}^{i-t,j},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right\rangle=0.

∎

Lemma 18
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, for any
t
such that
t<i\leq k
, if
t=o(k^{r/2})
and
B_{k}=o(k^{r/2})
, then as
k\rightarrow\infty
, we have

\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\right]\right|\rightarrow 0

\mbox{and}~~\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|\rightarrow 0

where
q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})=\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}
.

Proof.

By applying Assumption
A.1
and Lemma
2
, we have
q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\leq U_{L}M^{2}
.
Let

\Delta_{(i)}=\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})\right]\right|;

\Delta_{(ii)}=\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}[g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})]\right\|.

By applying Jensen’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{(i)}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.\left.{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right|\right]\leq U_{L}M^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Delta\right]

\displaystyle\Delta_{(ii)}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[\left.\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.\left.{\mathbb{E}}_{\boldsymbol{s}\sim\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right),\boldsymbol{a}\sim\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\cdot|\boldsymbol{s})}\left[\left.\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a};\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|\right|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right]\right|\right]\leq M{\mathbb{E}}\left[\Delta\right]

where the outer expectation is take over the transition probability from
\boldsymbol{s}_{1}
to
\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)}
and
\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}
and
\Delta=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})-\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})\right|\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}
. Then,

\displaystyle\Delta

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|\int_{\mathcal{A}}\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathcal{S}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})-\mathbb{P}(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t})\right\|_{TV}.

Consider the auxiliary MDP introduced in Section
C.1
, with
\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}
. Then decomposing the above total variation between
nt
-step and
nk
-step transition probability gives

\displaystyle\Delta

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\cdot)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\cdot)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}.

(112)

By applying the result in (
76
), the first and last term can be bounded by

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}t\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell};

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}

\displaystyle\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}.

By applying Assumption
A.1
, the second and third terms can be bounded by

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(k,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)-d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\cdot)\right\|_{TV}\leq\varphi\left(n(k-i+t)\right);

\displaystyle\left\|d^{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}}}(\cdot)-\mathbb{P}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{s}}^{(i,j)}\in\cdot|\boldsymbol{s}^{(i-t,j)},\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\right)\right\|_{TV}\leq\varphi\left(nt\right).

Due to
t<i\leq k
and
k-i\leq B_{k}
, where
B_{k}
is the buffer size, by choosing suitable
t=o\left(k^{r/2}\right)
and
B_{k}=o\left(k^{r/2}\right)
, we have, as
k\rightarrow\infty

\displaystyle\varphi(n(k-i+t))\leq\varphi(nt)

\displaystyle\rightarrow 0

\displaystyle t\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}=t(k-i+t)\eta_{i-t}\leq\eta_{1}\frac{B_{k}t+t^{2}}{(k-B_{k}-t)^{r}}

\displaystyle\rightarrow 0

\displaystyle t\sum^{i}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}=t^{2}\eta_{i-t}\leq\eta_{1}\frac{t^{2}}{(k-B_{k}-t)^{r}}

\displaystyle\rightarrow 0

which implies that
{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]\leq 2nU_{\pi}M^{2}\eta_{1}\frac{B_{k}t+2t^{2}}{(k-B_{k}-t)^{r}}+2\varphi(nt)\rightarrow 0
. Since
\Delta_{(ii)}\leq\Delta_{(i)}\leq U_{L}M^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\Delta]
, we have
\Delta_{(i)}\rightarrow 0
and
\Delta_{(ii)}\rightarrow 0
as
k\rightarrow\infty
.
∎

Lemma 19
.

Under the same condition as Proposition
2
.
Define
q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})=\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}
. If
\liminf_{k\rightarrow\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}=\underline{a}>0
, we have
\log\mathcal{G}\rightarrow 0
as
k\rightarrow\infty
.

Proof.

Consider that

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{G}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{({\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}-q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}]){\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}+({\mathbb{E}}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}.

By applying Assumption
A.1
and Lemma
2
, we have
{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})^{2}]\leq M^{4}
and further

\displaystyle q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})+q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})

\displaystyle\leq(U_{L}+1)M^{2}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]+{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]

\displaystyle\leq(U_{L}+1)M^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\log\mathcal{G}\leq\frac{M^{6}(U_{L}+1)\left({\mathbb{E}}_{k}[|q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})-q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})|]+|{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]-{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]|\right)}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}.

(113)

By applying Assumption
A.1
and Lemma
2
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[|q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})-q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})|]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\|g(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq M^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq M^{2}U_{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}\left[\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq M^{2}U_{L}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{A}}P\left(\boldsymbol{s}^{(k,j)}=\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{s}|\boldsymbol{s}_{1}\right)\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})\frac{|\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})-\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})|}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}}(\boldsymbol{a}|\boldsymbol{s})}\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{a}

\displaystyle\leq M^{2}U_{L}U_{\pi}\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i}\|\leq M^{3}U_{L}U_{\pi}\sum^{k}_{\ell=i}\eta_{i}

(114)

where (
114
) follows Lemma
20
.
Notice that

\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})-q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]\right|\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[|q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})-q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})|]\leq M^{3}U_{L}U_{\pi}\sum^{k}_{\ell=i}\eta_{i}.

By substituting it into Eq. (
113
), we have

\log\mathcal{G}\leq\frac{2M^{9}(U_{L}+1)U_{L}U_{\pi}\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}.

Because of
k-i\leq B_{k}
,
t<i\leq B_{k}\leq k
and
\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\leq\eta_{1}\frac{k-i+t}{(i-t)^{r}}{\leq}\eta_{1}\frac{B_{k}+t}{(k-B_{k}-t)^{r}}
, we have

\log\mathcal{G}\leq\frac{2M^{9}(U_{L}+1)U_{L}U_{\pi}\eta_{1}\frac{B_{k}+t}{(k-B_{k}-t)^{r}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}}.

By choosing a suitable buffer size and the lag term such that
B_{k}=o(k^{r})
and
t=o(k^{r})
,
we have
\log\mathcal{G}\rightarrow 0
provided that
\liminf_{k\rightarrow\infty}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{i,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{k}[q_{k,k}(\boldsymbol{s},\boldsymbol{a})]^{2}>0
.
∎

Appendix H
Proof of Auxillary Lemmas

Lemma 20
.

Under Assumption
A.1
and
A.2
, for any
k\geq i\geq t
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|]\leq M\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}{\eta_{\ell}}.

Proof.

By applying Minkowski inequality, it holds

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{k}-\boldsymbol{\theta}_{i-t}\|]

\displaystyle\leq\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{1}{|\mathcal{U}_{\ell}|n}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\in\mathcal{U}_{\ell}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}\left\|g\left(\boldsymbol{x}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell}\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq M\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}\eta_{\ell}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{U}_{\ell}|n}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\in\mathcal{U}_{\ell}}\sum^{n}_{j=1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\frac{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{\ell}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}{\pi_{\boldsymbol{\theta}_{h}}\left(\boldsymbol{a}^{(h,j)}|\boldsymbol{s}^{(h,j)}\right)}\right]

(115)

\displaystyle\leq M\sum^{k}_{\ell=i-t}{\eta_{\ell}}

where Step (
115
) holds by Lemma
2
.
∎

Lemma 21
.

Suppose
\eta_{k}=\eta_{1}k^{-r}
is a non-negative sequence for some positive constants
\eta_{1}
and
r
. Then for any large enough
k
and
\tau<k
, we have

\sum^{k-t}_{i=1}\eta_{i}\leq\frac{\eta_{1}}{1-r}(k-t)^{1-r}.

Proof.

The conclusion is obtained by observing that

\sum^{k-t}_{i=1}\eta_{i}=\sum^{k-t}_{i=1}\frac{\eta_{1}}{i^{r}}\leq\eta_{1}\int^{k-t}_{1}x^{-r}\mathrm{d}x=\eta_{1}\frac{(k-t)^{1-r}}{1-r}-\frac{\eta_{1}}{1-r}\leq\eta_{1}\frac{(k-t)^{1-r}}{1-r}

which completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 22
.

Suppose
\{a_{k}\}
is a non-negative, bounded sequence. Let
U_{a}
denote the bound of the sequence. Then
for any large enough
K
, let
\tau=f(K)<K
(i.e.
f(\cdot)
is a positive increasing function) and we have

\frac{1}{K}\sum^{K}_{k=1}a_{k}\leq\frac{\tau-1}{K}U_{a}+\frac{1}{1+K-\tau}\sum^{K}_{k=\tau}a_{k}.

Proof.

The conclusion is obtained by observing that

\frac{1}{K}\sum^{T}_{k=1}a_{k}\leq\frac{1}{K}\left((\tau-1)U_{a}+\sum^{K}_{k=\tau}a_{k}\right)=\frac{\tau-1}{K}U_{a}+\frac{1}{K}\sum^{K}_{k=\tau}a_{k}\leq\frac{\tau-1}{K}U_{a}+\frac{1}{1+K-\tau}\sum^{K}_{k=\tau}a_{k}.

∎
